2026-11-19T21:58:01.1048672Z [36;1m  }[0m
2026-11-19T21:58:01.1048899Z [36;1m  recover_result() {[0m

2026-11-19T21:58:01.1049931Z [36;1m    for ((attempt=0; attempt<3; attempt++)); do[0m

2026-11-19T21:58:01.1052535Z [36;1m      previous=()[0m
2026-11-19T21:58:01.1052858Z [36;1m      if [ "$before" != "$initial_identity" ]; then[0m

2026-11-19T21:58:01.1054173Z [36;1m        else[0m

2026-11-19T21:58:01.1058200Z [36;1m      if [ "$before" != "$after" ]; then[0m
2026-11-19T21:58:01.1058538Z [36;1m        sleep 1[0m
2026-11-19T21:58:01.1058795Z [36;1m        continue[0m
2026-11-19T21:58:01.1059058Z [36;1m      fi[0m

2026-11-19T21:58:01.1060342Z [36;1m        return[0m

2026-11-19T21:58:01.1061960Z [36;1m        exit 1[0m

2026-11-19T21:58:01.1063475Z [36;1m    exit 1[0m
